# Calls compared with MATLAB (see docs/signal-coverage-plan.md). One call per line; every line
# assigns `r`, a numeric array or a cell array of them, or fails with an error whose message is
# compared. `x`, `y`, `y2` (100-sample signals) and `t` are defined by gen_cases.py.

# cfirpm: response functions
fh = @(n,f,gf,w) deal(double(abs(gf)<0.5).*exp(-1j*pi*gf*n/2), ones(size(gf))); [b1,d1] = cfirpm(10,[-1 -0.5 -0.4 0.3 0.4 1],fh); r = {b1,d1};
